registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e

    ) -> None:

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")

        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None

                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"

                    )

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e
E                   sqlalchemy.exc.InvalidRequestError: One or more mappers failed to initialize - can't proceed with initialization of other mappers. Triggering mapper: 'Mapper[Batch(batches)]'. Original exception was: Mapper 'Mapper[AcademicYear(academic_years)]' has no property 'batches'.  If this property was indicated from other mappers or configure events, ensure registry.configure() has been called.

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e
E                   sqlalchemy.exc.InvalidRequestError: One or more mappers failed to initialize - can't proceed with initialization of other mappers. Triggering mapper: 'Mapper[Batch(batches)]'. Original exception was: Mapper 'Mapper[AcademicYear(academic_years)]' has no property 'batches'.  If this property was indicated from other mappers or configure events, ensure registry.configure() has been called.

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e

registries = set(), cascade = True

    @util.preload_module("sqlalchemy.orm.decl_api")
    def _do_configure_registries(
        registries: Set[_RegistryType], cascade: bool
    ) -> None:
        registry = util.preloaded.orm_decl_api.registry
    
        orig = set(registries)
    
        for reg in registry._recurse_with_dependencies(registries):
            has_skip = False
    
            for mapper in reg._mappers_to_configure():
                run_configure = None
    
                for fn in mapper.dispatch.before_mapper_configured:
                    run_configure = fn(mapper, mapper.class_)
                    if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                        has_skip = True
                        break
                if run_configure is EXT_SKIP:
                    continue
    
                if getattr(mapper, "_configure_failed", False):
                    e = sa_exc.InvalidRequestError(
                        "One or more mappers failed to initialize - "
                        "can't proceed with initialization of other "
                        "mappers. Triggering mapper: '%s'. "
                        "Original exception was: %s"
                        % (mapper, mapper._configure_failed)
                    )
                    e._configure_failed = mapper._configure_failed  # type: ignore
>                   raise e
